## Implemented acceptance evidence

- tests/test_application.py: staff HTTP decisions/signature/completion → actual services/PostgreSQL → immutable outbox → internal Finance adapter/inbox → cited synthetic agent run/audit, all sharing case/evidence IDs.
- WorkerConcurrencyTests.test_staff_handoff_survives_worker_process_exit_and_restart commits actual staff requests, starts a separate Python process that reserves a lease and exits, expires the persisted lease, then starts a fresh work_outbox process. It asserts one confirmed Finance receipt and a subsequent linked agent audit. This proves process-boundary recovery for that path; it does not establish every M1/FULL acceptance requirement.
- TransactionTestCase uses two actual PostgreSQL connections/threads to claim one intent once. Tests cover expired fencing, timeout after persisted acceptance, failed receipt lookup without dispatch, revoked-grant hold, semantic duplicate milestones and payload/receipt immutability.
- Exact costing sequence and separate quality decision; no premature signature; cost revision invalidation; policy revocation; supersession and reapproval without overwriting decision history.
- Per-record/action access, cross-case evidence exclusion, admin-without-grant denial, CSRF enforcement and template escaping. Source instructions stay quoted data and uncited provider output is rejected.
- All 57 components tested absent and invalid-draft activation; independently configured timezone/roles/source validators tested active, invalid, superseded, stale or revoked as applicable. Synthetic-to-operational mix is rejected. This is not a complete active/revoked golden fixture matrix for all 57; 53 semantic implementations remain unfinished.
- tests/test_finance_intake.py: exact decimal text and input lineage, unknown amount preservation, malformed/duplicate inputs, configured conventions, currency/period mismatch, tampered artifact hash, source-policy holds, actual staff capture screen and Finance preparation intake. ## Known environment failures / limitations

The initial default Python had no Django/psycopg. Sandboxed package downloads failed with WinError 10013; approved network downloads installed the locked local dependencies. Docker daemon was unavailable. Portable PostgreSQL initialization succeeded but pg_ctl could not create its restricted token in the sandbox; starting the same local instance through approved execution succeeded. These environment failures were resolved and are not passing application tests.

The original tests/acceptance.json remains a specification. No complete workflow-family or per-process acceptance is marked passed from these shared paths. Full task/timer/cancel/held-job recovery, actual source financial oracles, remaining policy semantics, real provider/adapters, operational security, restore/export/load and complete product UAT still require implementation and test evidence.
